# HELP go_cpu_classes_gc_pause_cpu_seconds_total Estimated total CPU time spent with the application paused by the GC. Even if only one thread is running during the pause, this is computed as GOMAXPROCS times the pause latency because nothing else can be executing. This is the exact sum of samples in /sched/pauses/total/gc:seconds if each sample is multiplied by GOMAXPROCS at the time it is taken. This metric is an overestimate, and not directly comparable to system CPU time measurements. Compare only with other /cpu/classes metrics. Sourced from /cpu/classes/gc/pause:cpu-seconds.
# TYPE go_cpu_classes_gc_pause_cpu_seconds_total counter
go_cpu_classes_gc_pause_cpu_seconds_total 0.005460816

# HELP go_gc_limiter_last_enabled_gc_cycle GC cycle the last time the GC CPU limiter was enabled. This metric is useful for diagnosing the root cause of an out-of-memory error, because the limiter trades memory for CPU time when the GC's CPU time gets too high. This is most likely to occur with use of SetMemoryLimit. The first GC cycle is cycle 1, so a value of 0 indicates that it was never enabled. Sourced from /gc/limiter/last-enabled:gc-cycle.

# HELP kueue_cluster_queue_weighted_share Reports a value that representing the maximum of the ratios of usage above nominal\nquota to the lendable resources in the cohort, among all the resources provided by\nthe ClusterQueue, and divided by the weight.\nIf zero, it means that the usage of the ClusterQueue is below the nominal quota.\nIf the ClusterQueue has a weight of zero and is borrowing, this will return NaN.
# TYPE kueue_cluster_queue_weighted_share gauge
kueue_cluster_queue_weighted_share{cluster_queue="team-a",cohort="all",replica_role="leader"} 250
kueue_cluster_queue_weighted_share{cluster_queue="team-b",cohort="all",replica_role="leader"} 250

# HELP kueue_evicted_workloads_once_total The number of unique workload evictions per 'cluster_queue',\nThe label 'reason' can have the following values:\n- "Preempted" means that the workload was evicted in order to free resources for a workload with a higher priority or reclamation of nominal quota.\n- "PodsReadyTimeout" means that the eviction took place due to a PodsReady timeout.\n- "AdmissionCheck" means that the workload was evicted because at least one admission check transitioned to False.\n- "ClusterQueueStopped" means that the workload was evicted because the ClusterQueue is stopped.\n- "LocalQueueStopped" means that the workload was evicted because the LocalQueue is stopped.\n- "NodeFailures" means that the workload was evicted due to node failures when using TopologyAwareScheduling.\n- "Deactivated" means that the workload was evicted because spec.active is set to false.\nThe label 'underlying_cause' can have the following values:\n- "" means that the value in 'reason' label is the root cause for eviction.\n- "WaitForStart" means that the pods have not been ready since admission, or the workload is not admitted.\n- "WaitForRecovery" means that the Pods were ready since the workload admission, but some pod has failed.\n- "WaitForScheduling" means that the workload was evicted by the PodsReady timeout while its PodsReady condition reported WaitForScheduling. This can include missing required Pods or a fallback to the regular readiness timeout.\n- "AdmissionCheck" means that the workload was evicted by Kueue due to a rejected admission check.\n- "MaximumExecutionTimeExceeded" means that the workload was evicted by Kueue due to maximum execution time exceeded.\n- "RequeuingLimitExceeded" means that the workload was evicted by Kueue due to requeuing limit exceeded.
# TYPE kueue_evicted_workloads_once_total counter
kueue_evicted_workloads_once_total{cluster_queue="team-a",priority_class="",reason="Preempted",replica_role="leader",underlying_cause=""} 1
kueue_evicted_workloads_once_total{cluster_queue="team-b",priority_class="",reason="Preempted",replica_role="leader",underlying_cause=""} 1
# HELP kueue_evicted_workloads_total The number of evicted workloads per 'cluster_queue',\nThe label 'reason' can have the following values:\n- "Preempted" means that the workload was evicted in order to free resources for a workload with a higher priority or reclamation of nominal quota.\n- "PodsReadyTimeout" means that the eviction took place due to a PodsReady timeout.\n- "AdmissionCheck" means that the workload was evicted because at least one admission check transitioned to False.\n- "ClusterQueueStopped" means that the workload was evicted because the ClusterQueue is stopped.\n- "LocalQueueStopped" means that the workload was evicted because the LocalQueue is stopped.\n- "NodeFailures" means that the workload was evicted due to node failures when using TopologyAwareScheduling.\n- "Deactivated" means that the workload was evicted because spec.active is set to false.\nThe label 'underlying_cause' can have the following values:\n- "" means that the value in 'reason' label is the root cause for eviction.\n- "WaitForStart" means that the pods have not been ready since admission, or the workload is not admitted.\n- "WaitForRecovery" means that the Pods were ready since the workload admission, but some pod has failed.\n- "WaitForScheduling" means that the workload was evicted by the PodsReady timeout while its PodsReady condition reported WaitForScheduling. This can include missing required Pods or a fallback to the regular readiness timeout.\n- "AdmissionCheck" means that the workload was evicted by Kueue due to a rejected admission check.\n- "MaximumExecutionTimeExceeded" means that the workload was evicted by Kueue due to maximum execution time exceeded.\n- "RequeuingLimitExceeded" means that the workload was evicted by Kueue due to requeuing limit exceeded.
# TYPE kueue_evicted_workloads_total counter
kueue_evicted_workloads_total{cluster_queue="team-a",priority_class="",reason="Preempted",replica_role="leader",underlying_cause=""} 1
kueue_evicted_workloads_total{cluster_queue="team-b",priority_class="",reason="Preempted",replica_role="leader",underlying_cause=""} 1

# HELP kueue_local_queue_evicted_workloads_total The number of evicted workloads per 'local_queue',\nThe label 'reason' can have the following values:\n- "Preempted" means that the workload was evicted in order to free resources for a workload with a higher priority or reclamation of nominal quota.\n- "PodsReadyTimeout" means that the eviction took place due to a PodsReady timeout.\n- "AdmissionCheck" means that the workload was evicted because at least one admission check transitioned to False.\n- "ClusterQueueStopped" means that the workload was evicted because the ClusterQueue is stopped.\n- "LocalQueueStopped" means that the workload was evicted because the LocalQueue is stopped.\n- "NodeFailures" means that the workload was evicted due to node failures when using TopologyAwareScheduling.\n- "Deactivated" means that the workload was evicted because spec.active is set to false.\nThe label 'underlying_cause' can have the following values:\n- "" means that the value in 'reason' label is the root cause for eviction.\n- "WaitForStart" means that the pods have not been ready since admission, or the workload is not admitted.\n- "WaitForRecovery" means that the Pods were ready since the workload admission, but some pod has failed.\n- "WaitForScheduling" means that the workload was evicted by the PodsReady timeout while its PodsReady condition reported WaitForScheduling. This can include missing required Pods or a fallback to the regular readiness timeout.\n- "AdmissionCheck" means that the workload was evicted by Kueue due to a rejected admission check.\n- "MaximumExecutionTimeExceeded" means that the workload was evicted by Kueue due to maximum execution time exceeded.\n- "RequeuingLimitExceeded" means that the workload was evicted by Kueue due to requeuing limit exceeded.
# TYPE kueue_local_queue_evicted_workloads_total counter
kueue_local_queue_evicted_workloads_total{name="lq",namespace="team-a",priority_class="",reason="Preempted",replica_role="leader",underlying_cause=""} 1
kueue_local_queue_evicted_workloads_total{name="lq",namespace="team-b",priority_class="",reason="Preempted",replica_role="leader",underlying_cause=""} 1
